Boring Dress Women's Natural Organic Cotton Herbal Dyed Long Zip Pregnant/Prenatal & Post Maternity Pink Nighty/Nightwear/Sleepwear/Night GownChemical dyeing processes use over 8000 different chemicals in its various stages. As high as 40 times the weight of the fabric, water is required to process the same. Most of the so called eco-friendly dyeing or LOW impact dyeing available on organic as well as conventional cotton is still being dyed by using huge amounts of chemicals and dyes but in permissible limits. Thus they may be LOW impact but certainly not NO impact. The Eco cycle or ORGANIC Textiles can only end with True Organic processing where the waste is reused for e.g. in the form of manure.0728UP0KS1MOCO1011109902018-07-28 23:34:57 ISTy111B07FZ9LSBYB07FZ9LSBY0DEFAULTInactive1499.0
